# Summary

Index session event windows

## Question

Which admitted source snapshots contain visible message records in each harvest partition, and which source frontiers remain unprocessed?

## Scope

The exact1659-row CA-A-905 v1 snapshot and unchanged [2026-09-04 05:54:15 +0400,2026-10-04 05:54:15 +0400) window. Source row numbers below are one-based positions in its durable embedded JSON. The single historical alias candidate remains unread under CA-C-294.

## Approach

1. Read each admitted source only through its declared snapshot byte length; hash the observed prefix and preserve exact byte/line boundaries.
2. Index response_item user/assistant messages, excluding event_msg mirrors, Tool/nonmessage records, internal analysis and machine environment-context records. Primary and worker origins remain separate; neither origin nor a timestamp establishes a new Operator decision.
3. Assign each indexed message to exactly one half-open partition: September4–12,12–20,20–28,28–October4, all at05:54:15+0400. Preserve continuation files without semantic deduplication.
4. Keep detailed record fingerprints/offsets in disposable .caprmedio_tmp/CA-P-1117/event-window-index.json as a retrieval accelerator. The original sources and durable metadata manifest remain retrievable evidence, and this Analysis retains every source snapshot disposition.
5. Apply8GiB aggregate,64MiB record and480-second bounds. Actual scan read6604133498 bytes in37.6923 seconds; no bound or read/parse error was reached.

## Results

1658 admitted source snapshots were indexed; one candidate remains identity_pending_unread. Primary visible-message counts by partition:1949,1826,1398,1415 (6588 total). Worker counts:474,476,1422,4051 (6423 total). These are indexed records, not reviewed decisions or harvested content; copied/contextual material still requires interpretation and supersession disposition.

Earlier packet preflight counts used different environment-context selection. The original main source's refined counts are1869 and624, rather than1877 and627; the same-ID continuation's second-partition count is759 rather than763. The exact bound packet records remain unchanged. Machine-generated environment_context messages are not independent human decisions. Third-partition continuation1175 already used the same exclusion. Subsequent packet generation must use one declared predicate and retain explicit exclusions.

Independent root verification compared every1659 durable table row with the detailed index and checked all13011 message offsets/partition counts. A separate native-prefix scan confirmed machine environment-context exclusions by partition: original main8/3/0/0; continuation0/4/9/14. This proves the earlier count differences without changing any previously bound substantive packet. All checks passed; this is index integrity evidence, not semantic harvest coverage.

Snapshot hashes describe the declared observed prefixes, not perpetually immutable appendable files. No later appended data is claimed covered. Source creation/modification dates are not substitute event-window evidence. A completed index neither closes the four substantive harvest partitions nor unlocks methodology/implementation.
